\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}=\bigcap^{3}_{j=1}\zeta_{j}
and
\delta_{4}=\Pr(\neg\zeta_{4})
.
We first consider the first term
\|\mathbf{T}_{1}\|
.
Letting
\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}=1+\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}
and
\bar{\gamma}_{i}=1+\frac{1}{m\pi_{i}}F_{ii}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}
with
\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}
the
i^{\text{th}}
row of
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}
, along with the fact that the event
\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}
indicates
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}=\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
and
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}=\mathbf{I}_{p}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}_{1}\|

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right)\right]\right\|=\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}2\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}2\|\mathbf{r}\|\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 16\|\mathbf{r}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right],

where in
(a)
we apply Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, in
(b)
we use
Lemma

D.2
.
Recalling the result (
25
) in
Lemma

D.1
, we then get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}_{1}\|=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|.

We next explore the another term
\|\mathbf{T}_{2}\|
. Using the fact
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}=0
, we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}_{2}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}]\|=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{4}}(\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]\|)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{4}}(\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]\|)=-\frac{1}{1-\delta_{4}}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]\|=\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]

\displaystyle\leq\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}2\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\check{a}^{2}_{s}]}

\displaystyle\leq\max\limits_{\|\mathbf{u}\|=1}2\|\mathbf{r}\|\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]}.

Taking
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}=\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{-s}\mathbf{H}^{1/2}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
again, we derive

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}]={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}]\leq 64,

and

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u})^{2}]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{1}{\pi_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 8^{4}\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{\pi_{i}}\leq 8^{4}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p.

Applying the Chebyshev’s inequality, we obtain, for
z>64
,

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime}})\leq\frac{8^{4}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{z^{2}}.

This together with
\delta_{4}\leq m^{-3}
further leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}]=\int^{\infty}_{0}\Pr(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime}})dz

\displaystyle=\int^{2m^{2}}_{0}\Pr(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime}})dz+\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}}\Pr(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime}})dz

\displaystyle\leq 2m^{2}\delta_{4}+\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}}\Pr(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{u}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime}})dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{m}+\frac{8^{4}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{2m^{2}}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m^{2}}\right).

We thus get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}_{2}\|=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|.

D.3
Detailed proof of the variance of debiased estimator in Theorem
4.4

A heuristic derivation of the variance of the debiased estimator proceeds analogously to that of the bias, and is omitted here for clarity.

Building on the proof strategy for the bias of the debiased estimator, the analysis of its variance proceeds in two main steps:

1.

construct a high probability event
\zeta
as in (
14
); and

2.

conditioning on that event
\zeta
, bound the quantity
\textbf{Var}(\check{\boldsymbol{\beta}})-\Delta(\mathbf{X})
using a “leave-one-out” type analysis.

To complete the analysis of the variance of the debiased estimator,
we begin by rewriting

\displaystyle~\textbf{Var}(\check{\boldsymbol{\beta}})-\Delta(\mathbf{X})={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{OLS})^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\check{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{OLS})]-\Delta(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{r}]-\Delta(\mathbf{X})=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum^{m}_{s=1}\sum^{m}_{k=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}]-\Delta(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum^{m}_{s=k=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}^{2}]+\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}]-\Delta(\mathbf{X}).

Recalling

\displaystyle\Delta(\mathbf{X})=\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\Big\{\frac{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r},

we then consider

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum^{m}_{s=k=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}^{2}]-\Delta(\mathbf{X})=\sum^{m}_{s=k=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}^{2}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}\right]-\Delta(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}^{2}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}\right]-\Delta(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{1}}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}-\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]}_{W_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}-\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}}_{W_{3}}+\underbrace{\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\Big\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}-\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})-\check{\Delta}_{\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2},i}}{m\pi_{i}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}}_{W_{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\Big\{\frac{\check{\Delta}_{\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2},i}}{m\pi_{i}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}}_{W_{5}},

where
\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}=1+F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{i_{s}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{i_{s}}
,
\check{\Delta}_{\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2},i}=\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}
, with diagonal matrix
\bar{\mathbf{F}}=\diag\{\bar{F}_{ii}\}^{n}_{i=1}
and diagonal entries

\bar{F}_{ii}=\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}.

In the following, we bound the term
W_{1}
. Considering
\mathbf{F}_{i_{s}i_{s}}<2
under the condition
m\geq 2\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p
, together with
Lemma

D.2
,
we get

\displaystyle W_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}-1\right|\right]\leq\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]+\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]+\frac{2}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{6}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-1\right|\right]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{6}{m\pi_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right],

which combined with
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
,
\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}/(m\pi_{i})\leq 8\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p/m
, and
Lemma

D.1
gives

\displaystyle W_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{384\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[r_{i}^{2}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right]\leq\frac{384\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\sum^{n}_{i=1}r_{i}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{384\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right]=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Next, we explore the term
W_{2}
,

\displaystyle W_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\delta_{4}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}-\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{1-\delta_{4}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}-\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right)\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\right]+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\right]+\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\delta_{4}}{m}

\displaystyle=2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\right]+O\left(\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m^{2}}\right)\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Using
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\right]=\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{1}{m\pi_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}^{2}\right]\leq\frac{64\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[(\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2})^{2}\right]\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{1}{m^{2}\pi_{i}^{3}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}^{2})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8^{4}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{r_{i}^{4}}{\ell_{i}}\leq\frac{8^{4}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{2}\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{4},

where we denote
\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})=\min\limits_{1\leq i\leq m,\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})\neq 0}\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})
. Following the Chebyshev’s inequality results in, for
z>128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}/m
, we get

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\geq z~|~\zeta^{\prime}\right)\leq\frac{8^{4}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{2}\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})z^{2}}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{4}.

Considering

\displaystyle\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})=\min\limits_{i,\|\mathbf{x}_{i}\|>0}\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{i}\geq\min\limits_{i,\|\mathbf{x}_{i}\|>0}\frac{\|\mathbf{x}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|\mathbf{X}\|^{2}}\geq\min\limits_{i,\|\mathbf{x}_{i}\|>0}\frac{\|\mathbf{x}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|\mathbf{X}\|_{F}^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{n^{\alpha}},

for some constant
\alpha>0
, together with
\delta_{4}\leq n^{-2-\alpha}
,
we further derive

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\right]

\displaystyle=\int^{\infty}_{0}\Pr\left(\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{\prime}\right)dz

\displaystyle=\int^{2m\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}/\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})}_{0}\Pr\left(\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{\prime}\right)dz

\displaystyle+\int^{\infty}_{2m\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}/\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})}\Pr\left(\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta_{4}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{\prime}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2m\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\delta_{4}}{\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})}+\int^{\infty}_{2m\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}/\ell_{\min}(\mathbf{X})}\Pr\left(\frac{1}{m}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}^{2}\geq z~|~\zeta^{\prime}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq 2mn^{\alpha}\delta_{4}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}+\frac{8^{4}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{2m^{3}}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

This further yields

\displaystyle W_{2}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Recalling the result (
24
) in
Lemma

D.1
, it follows that

\displaystyle W_{3}\leq\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}-\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]\|=O\left(\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Then, applying the result (
23
) in
Lemma

D.1
leads to

\displaystyle W_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]-\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\right\|

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}}\cdot\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Then, we turn to bound the term
W_{5}
. Taking
\check{\mathbf{Q}}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
and

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\|\leq\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\bar{F}_{ii}\leq\frac{64\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m},

it follows that

\displaystyle W_{5}\leq\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\right\|=O\left(\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Now, it remains to bound the cross term
m^{-2}\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}]
. Without loss of generality, assume that the events
\zeta_{1}
,
\zeta_{2}
are independent of both
\mathbf{z}_{s}
and
\mathbf{z}_{k}
. Define
\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}=\zeta_{1}\bigcap\zeta_{2}
,
\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}=\zeta_{3}\bigcap\zeta_{4}
and
\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}=\Pr(\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}})
.
Let
\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}=1+\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}
.
We first rewrite

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}]=\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}\right]=\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]-\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]-\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{6}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]}_{W_{7}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{8}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]}_{W_{9}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}\frac{1}{1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{10}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}\frac{1}{1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]}_{W_{11}}.

Next, we first bound the term
W_{6}
. Define
\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}=1+\frac{1}{m\pi_{j}}F_{jj}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}
and
\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}=1+\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}
. We rewrite

\displaystyle W_{6}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{61}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}}\right)\right]}_{W_{62}}.

For the term
W_{61}
, we get

\displaystyle W_{61}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{611}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{612}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}\right]}_{W_{613}}.

Denote
\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}=1+\frac{1}{m\pi_{i}}F_{ii}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}
. Together with
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
and
F_{jj}<2
,
we then obtain

\displaystyle W_{611}

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F_{jj}r_{i}r_{j}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}\right\}^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\diag\left\{\left(\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right)\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 256{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

where the last line follows from (
26
) in
Lemma

D.1
. Using
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}=0
,
we next consider the term
W_{612}
:

\displaystyle W_{612}

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}r_{j}\left(\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\left(\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right)\right\}^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}\right]=0.

Recalling
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}=0
again, we now consider
W_{613}
:

\displaystyle W_{613}=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}r_{j}\right]=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}\right]=0.

Now, we turn to explore the term
W_{62}
. Noting
1<F_{ii}<2
,
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
, and

\displaystyle\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}=1+\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}}{m}-\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\geq 1/2,

under
m\geq 256\theta_{\max}p
,
we get

\displaystyle W_{62}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F^{2}_{ii}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r_{i}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}_{i}\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F^{2}_{ii}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{2}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}_{i}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{ii}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{2}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}_{i}\pi^{1/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right]+\frac{8192\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{a}^{2}_{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\times 8^{6}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}r^{2}_{i}+\frac{8192\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}r^{2}_{j}=O\left(\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

(16)

where in
(a)
we use the inequality
2xy\leq x^{2}+y^{2}
. Thus, we conclude

\displaystyle W_{6}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Next, we proceed to analyze the term
W_{7}
. Applying

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\right]\leq 4{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[|\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{a}^{2}_{s}\check{a}_{k}^{2}\right]}=4\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 4\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}\sqrt{\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\right]}\leq 256p^{1/2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2},

(17)

and

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{2}F_{ii}^{2}F_{jj}^{2}r^{2}_{i}r^{2}_{j}}{\pi_{j}\pi_{i}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{16}{\pi_{j}\pi_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{2}r^{2}_{i}r^{2}_{j}\right]\leq 2\times 8^{5}\theta_{\max}^{2}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{4},

(18)

together with the Chebyshev’s inequality, it follows that for
z>512p^{1/2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\right)\leq\frac{2\times 8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{4}}{z^{2}}.

With
\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\leq m^{-3}
, we then deduce

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\int^{\infty}_{0}\Pr\left(\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\right)dz

\displaystyle=\int^{2m^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}_{0}\Pr\left(\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\right)dz

\displaystyle+\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}\Pr\left(\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}+\int^{\infty}_{2m^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}\Pr\left(\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\neg\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\geq z~|~\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}+\frac{2\times 8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{2m^{2}}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

This thus gives

\displaystyle W_{7}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Subsequently, we continue to bound the term
W_{8}
. We get

\displaystyle W_{8}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{81}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}}-\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}}\right)\right]}_{W_{82}}.

In the following, we consider the first term
W_{81}
:

\displaystyle W_{81}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{811}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{3}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}\left(\frac{F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{812}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{3}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}\right]}_{W_{813}}.

Recalling
F_{ii}\leq 2
and
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
again, together with the result (
26
) in
Lemma

D.1
, we get

\displaystyle W_{811}

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F^{2}_{jj}r_{i}r_{j}}{m\pi_{j}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}^{2}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F_{jj}^{2}r_{i}r_{j}}{m\pi_{j}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}^{2}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{F_{jj}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}}{m\pi_{j}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}^{2}}\right\}^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4096\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Now, we turn to analyze the term
W_{812}
. Applying
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}=0
again leads to

\displaystyle W_{812}=\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}r_{j}}{m\pi_{j}}\left(\frac{F_{jj}^{2}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}^{2}}-1\right)\right]=0.

Similarly, we get

\displaystyle W_{813}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{1}{m\pi_{j}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r_{i}r_{j}\right]=0.

We then consider the term
W_{82}
, with
F_{ii}<2
and
\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\preceq 8\mathbf{I}_{p}
,

\displaystyle W_{82}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F^{2}_{ii}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r_{i}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}_{i}\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F^{2}_{ii}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{3}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}_{i}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{ii}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{3}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}_{i}\pi^{1/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16384\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{16384\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

(19)

We now proceed to bound the term
W_{9}
. Similar to (
17
) and (
18
),
we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\right]\leq\frac{512\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[|\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{512\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{a}^{2}_{s}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{512\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{512\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}\sqrt{\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\right]}\leq\frac{4096\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3/2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m},

and

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\left(\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{2}F^{2}_{ii}F^{4}_{jj}r^{2}_{i}r^{2}_{j}}{m^{2}\pi^{3}_{j}\pi_{i}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}^{4}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 64\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\frac{1}{m^{2}\pi^{3}_{j}\pi_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{2}r^{2}_{i}r^{2}_{j}\right]\leq\frac{8^{8}\theta_{\max}^{4}(\mathbf{X})p^{4}}{m^{2}}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{4},

Similarly to the bound of
W_{7}
, we obtain

\displaystyle W_{9}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta_{\max}^{4}(\mathbf{X})p^{4}}{m^{4}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

In the following, we turn to explore the term
W_{10}
. We first rewrite

\displaystyle W_{10}

\displaystyle=\sum^{m}_{s\neq k}\frac{1}{1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{101}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s}}}-\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}\right)\right]}_{W_{102}}.

Then, we write the term
W_{101}
:

\displaystyle W_{101}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{1011}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{1012}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m}\right]}_{W_{1013}}.

For the term
W_{1013}
, we have

\displaystyle W_{1013}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{10131}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m}\right]}_{W_{10132}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{2}}\right]}_{W_{10133}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]}_{W_{10134}}.

(20)

Applying (
16
) yields

\displaystyle W_{10131}

\displaystyle=W_{10134}=-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Taking the fact
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}=0
, we further obtain

\displaystyle W_{10132}=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]=0.

Recalling (
19
), we
bound the term
W_{10133}
:

\displaystyle W_{10133}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

We thus conclude

\displaystyle W_{1013}=O\left(\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Subsequently, we continue to bound the term
W_{1011}
.
We rewrite

\displaystyle W_{1011}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10111}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10112}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{3}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10113}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10114}}.

Following (
26
) in
Lemma

D.1
and the bound on (
19
), we explore the terms
W_{10111}
and
W_{10114}
:

\displaystyle W_{10111}=W_{10114}=-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r_{i}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi_{i}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}^{2}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}^{2}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{1/2}_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}^{2}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|+\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{1/2}_{i}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|+\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+3\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{1/2}_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{24\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{24\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{96\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{24\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{96\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1536\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12\times 8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]+\frac{6144\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{a}^{2}_{k}\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12\times 8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]+\frac{6144\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{7}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{7}}{m^{7}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

(21)

Similarly, we get

\displaystyle W_{10113}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}^{3}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{3}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{s}i_{s}}^{2}}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{9}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{9}}{m^{9}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

We then derive

\displaystyle W_{10112}=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}\right\}^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\left(\frac{F_{ii}^{2}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}}-1\right)\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{256\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}^{2}}{\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{256\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{768\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

(22)

Thus, we get

\displaystyle W_{1011}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Now, we turn to bound the term
W_{1012}
:

\displaystyle W_{1012}

\displaystyle=-\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10121}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10122}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{2}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10123}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]}_{W_{10124}}.

We begin by bounding the term
W_{10121}
.
Using (
26
) in
Lemma

D.1
and (
D.3
), we get

\displaystyle W_{10121}

\displaystyle=W_{10124}=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}r_{i}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}\pi_{i}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}\pi^{1/2}_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]
