\displaystyle+\frac{8\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F_{jj}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\left|\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{8\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}^{2}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{512\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\times 8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right|\right]+\frac{512\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\left|\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{7}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{7}}{m^{7}}}\right)\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Analogously, we derive

\displaystyle W_{10123}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}^{2}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{2}}\left(\frac{F_{i_{k}i_{k}}}{\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{9}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{9}}{m^{9}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Recalling
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{r}=\mathbf{0}
, together with the bound on
W_{10112}
in (
22
), we attain

\displaystyle W_{10122}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{r}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right\}^{n}_{j=1}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\diag\left\{\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}}{m\pi_{i}}\right\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{128\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{jj}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{s,k,j}}}-1\right|\right]=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Then, we get

\displaystyle W_{1012}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Consequently, it follows that

\displaystyle W_{101}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\cdot\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Now, we proceed to explore the term
W_{102}
:

\displaystyle W_{102}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\left(\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s}}}-\frac{1}{\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{2\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{3}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}}{m^{3}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}_{i_{k,s}}}\right]}_{W_{1021}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{4}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}(\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s})^{2}}{m^{5}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{k,s}}}\right]}_{W_{1022}}.

Considering the bound on
W_{82}
in (
19
), we have

\displaystyle W_{1021}

\displaystyle=\frac{2m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}F^{3}_{ii}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r_{i}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{3}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\pi_{i}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{1/2}F^{3}_{ii}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{3}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\pi^{5/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{3}_{ii}F^{2}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}}{m^{3}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}\check{\gamma}^{2}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\pi^{3/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{8^{5}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\right]=O\left(\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Similarly, we bound the term
W_{1022}
:

\displaystyle W_{1022}

\displaystyle=\frac{m(m-1)}{m^{2}(1-\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{2}F^{4}_{ii}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r_{i}\check{a}_{k}}{m^{5}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\pi_{i}^{3}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}^{\top}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{3/2}F^{4}_{ii}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}r^{2}_{i}}{m^{5}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\pi^{7/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{F^{4}_{ii}F^{3}_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}(\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}})^{5/2}}{m^{5}\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}^{2}\check{\gamma}^{3}_{i_{s,k}}\bar{\gamma}^{2}_{i}\pi^{5/2}_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\times 8^{7}\theta^{4}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{4}}{m^{4}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}r^{2}_{i}}{m\pi_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2\times 8^{7}\theta^{4}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{4}}{m^{4}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{a}^{2}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{i}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}}{m}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\theta^{5}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{5}}{m^{5}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

This yields

\displaystyle W_{102}=O\left(\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3}}{m^{3}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

We further conclude

\displaystyle W_{10}=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\cdot\frac{\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

Now, it remains to derive the bound of the term
W_{11}
.
Recalling (
17
) and (
18
) again,
we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\left|\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right|\right]\leq\frac{8^{3}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[|\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8^{3}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p}{m}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{a}^{2}_{s}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{8^{3}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{8^{3}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m}\sqrt{\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\mathbf{x}_{\mathbf{H}_{j}}\right]}\leq\frac{8^{4}\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{3/2}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}}{m},

and

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k}}{m\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}}\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\frac{(\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}^{2}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}F^{2}_{i_{s}i_{s}}F_{i_{k}i_{k}}\check{a}_{s}\check{a}_{k})^{2}}{m^{2}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s}}^{4}\check{\gamma}_{i_{s,k}}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8^{6}\theta^{2}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{2}}{m^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-sk}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{a}^{2}_{s}\check{a}^{2}_{k}\right]\leq\frac{8^{8}\theta^{4}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{4}}{m^{2}}\|\mathbf{r}\|^{4}.

Following the bound on the term
W_{7}
,
we similarly obtain, for
\delta_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\leq m^{-3}
,

\displaystyle W_{11}=O\left(\frac{1}{m}+\frac{\theta^{4}_{\max}(\mathbf{X})p^{4}}{m^{4}}\right)\cdot\|\mathbf{r}\|^{2}.

We thus complete the proof.

D.4
Proof of Corollary
4.5

The proof of
Corollary

4.5
is based on the same proof strategy developed for
Theorem

4.4
. For completeness, we briefly outline the necessary modifications.
The only substantive departure from the proof of
Theorem

4.4
lies in the auxiliary estimates used to control several key terms. In the leverage-score sampling setting, we rely on the refined bounds
\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]-\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\right\|
,
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}]\|
,
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right]
and
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]
, which are established in
Lemma

D.3
.
Once these bounds are invoked, the remainder of the argument proceeds identically to that of
Theorem

4.4
.

D.5
Proof of Corollary
4.7

The proof of
Corollary

4.7
follows the same overall strategy as that of
Theorem

4.4
. While the general structure of the argument remains unchanged, the technical distinctions arise from the auxiliary bounds employed. In the SRHT setting, the required estimates for
\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]-\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\right\|
,
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}^{2}_{-s}]\|
,
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i}}-1\right|\right]
and
{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}}\left[\max\limits_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\frac{F_{ii}}{\bar{\gamma}_{i_{k,s,i}}}-1\right|\right]
, are taken from
Lemma

D.4
.
Aside from substituting these bounds, the remainder of the proof follows the same sequence of arguments as in
Theorem

4.4
.

(

]
)
.

, suppose that the events
\zeta_{1}
,
\zeta_{2}
,

are independent of
\mathbf{z}_{s}

\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}=\bigcap^{3}_{j=1}\zeta_{j}
and
\delta_{4}=\Pr(\neg\zeta_{4})
.
For a p.s.d. random matrix
\mathbf{K}
(or any non-negative random variable) depending only on random sampling
\mathbf{S}
, we obtain,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{K}]=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[(\prod^{4}_{j=1}\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{j}})\mathbf{K}]}{\Pr(\zeta)}\preceq\frac{1}{1-\delta}{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{1}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}\mathbf{K}]\preceq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta^{{}^{\prime}}}[\mathbf{K}],

(27)

where
\mathbf{1}_{\zeta_{j}}
is the indicator of the event
\zeta_{j}
.

Appendix E
Proof of Theorem
3.1

We begin by recalling some notations used in
Appendix

D
.
Let
\check{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{s}=\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s}}/\sqrt{\pi_{i_{s}}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
with
\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}=\mathbf{X}\mathbf{H}^{-1/2}
and
\mathbf{H}=\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}
, and let

\check{\mathbf{Q}}=(\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}})^{-1}=\left(\sum^{m}_{s=1}\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{s},i_{s}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{s}\check{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{s}\right)^{-1},~~\check{\mathbf{Q}}_{-s}=\left(\sum_{k\neq s}\frac{1}{m}F_{i_{k},i_{k}}\check{\mathbf{z}}_{k}\check{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{k}\right)^{-1}.

Note that for
\mathrm{rank}(\mathbf{X})=p
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}(\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}=\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}},~~\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\dagger}=\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top},

which implies

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{E}[\mathbf{X}(\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}]-\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\dagger}\|^{2}_{F}=\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}]\|^{2}_{F}=\sum^{n}_{l=1}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{r}_{l}]\|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{X}(\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}-\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\dagger}\|^{2}_{F}]=\mathbf{E}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{-1}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}_{\zeta}[\|\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\|^{2}_{F}]=\sum^{n}_{l=1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{r}_{l}\|^{2}]

with
\mathbf{r}_{l}
the
l^{\text{th}}
column of
\mathbf{P}_{\perp}
.
Following the same line of arguments as in the proof of
Theorem

4.4
, we obtain that, for each column
\mathbf{r}_{l}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{r}_{l}]\|^{2}=\epsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{r}_{l}\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\check{\mathbf{Q}}\mathbf{X}_{\mathbf{H}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{r}_{l}\|^{2}]=\mathbf{r}_{l}^{\top}\diag\Big\{\frac{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}_{l}+\epsilon\|\mathbf{r}_{l}\|^{2}_{2}.

This implies

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{E}[\mathbf{X}(\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}]-\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\dagger}\|^{2}_{F}=\epsilon^{2}\sum^{n}_{l=1}\|\mathbf{r}_{l}\|^{2}=\epsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\|^{2}_{F},

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{X}(\check{\mathbf{S}}\mathbf{X})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}-\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\dagger}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle=\sum^{n}_{l=1}\mathbf{r}_{l}^{\top}\diag\Big\{\frac{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{r}_{l}+\epsilon\sum^{n}_{l=1}\|\mathbf{r}_{l}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\tr\left(\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\diag\Big\{\frac{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{X})}{m\pi_{i}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}\right)+\epsilon\|\mathbf{P}_{\perp}\|^{2}_{F}.

This completes the proof.

Appendix F
The full bias-variance characterizations on fast CUR

In this section, we provide the full bias–variance characterizations in
Theorem

F.1
, supplementing
Theorem

4.8
with the corresponding variance result. We then prove
Theorem

F.1
in
Section

F.1
, which also includes the proof of
Theorem

4.8
. Additional results related to
Theorem

F.1
are provided in
Section

F.2
.

Before showing
Theorem

F.1
, we first present
Algorithm

1
, which summarizes the fast CUR decomposition procedure and the computation of the standard sampled CUR estimator
\tilde{\mathbf{U}}
.

Algorithm 1

Fast CUR Decomposition
[
53
]

Input:

\mathbf{X}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times p}
,
\mathbf{C}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times c}
consisting of
c
columns of
\mathbf{X}
,
\mathbf{R}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times p}
consisting of
r
rows of
\mathbf{X}
, sample sizes
m_{c}
and
m_{r}
, sampling probabilities
\{\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}\}^{n}_{i=1}
and
\{\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}\}^{p}_{j=1}
with
\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}\geq 0
,
\sum^{n}_{i=1}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}=1
and
\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}\geq 0
,
\sum^{p}_{j=1}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}=1
.

Output:

\tilde{\mathbf{U}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{c\times r}
.

1.

Draw sampling matrices
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{c}\times n}
with probabilities
\{\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}\}^{n}_{i=1}
and
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{r}\times p}
with probabilities
\{\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}\}^{n}_{j=1}
as in
Definition

2.1
;

2.

Compute the sampled matrices
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{C}
,
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{R}^{\top}
,
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{S}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R}}
;

3.

Compute the solution of the sketched problem
\min\limits_{\mathbf{U}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X})\mathbf{S}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R}}\|_{F}^{2}
as

\tilde{\mathbf{U}}=(\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{C})^{\dagger}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{S}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{R}\mathbf{S}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}.

(28)

In the following, we provide precise statistical characterizations of the proposed debiased solution
\check{\mathbf{U}}
.

Theorem F.1

(
Precise characterizations for debiased fast CUR decomposition
)
.

For a data matrix
\mathbf{X}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times p}
, let
\mathbf{C}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times c}
and
\mathbf{R}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times p}
be the pre-selected columns and rows of
\mathbf{X}
, and let
\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}
be defined as in (
9
). Assume that
\min_{i,\|\mathbf{c}_{i}\|>0}\|\mathbf{c}_{i}\|^{2}/\|\mathbf{C}\|_{F}^{2}\geq n^{-{\alpha_{c}}}
and
\min_{j,\|\mathbf{r}_{j}\|>0}\|\mathbf{r}_{j}\|^{2}/\|\mathbf{R}\|_{F}^{2}\geq p^{-{\alpha_{r}}}
for some constants
\alpha_{c}>0
and
\alpha_{r}>0
.
For standard random sampling matrices
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}
and
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}
used in
Algorithm

1
, define their corresponding
debiased
sampling matrices
\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{c}\times n}
and
\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{r}\times p}
as in (
1
).
Then, there exists
C>0
independent of
n,p,c,r
so that for
m_{c}\geq C\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c\log(c/\delta)
and
m_{r}\geq C\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r\log(r/\delta)
,
\delta\leq\min\{n^{-(3+\alpha_{c})},p^{-(3+\alpha_{r})}\}
, when conditioned on an event
\zeta
that holds with probability at least
1-\delta
, the debiased matrix
\check{\mathbf{U}}
defined in (
11
) satisfies

\Bias_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})=\epsilon^{2}\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}),\quad\Var_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})=\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}+\epsilon\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}),

with
\epsilon=O(\sqrt{(\log n/\loglog n)^{2}\cdot\theta_{\max}^{3}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}/m_{c}^{3}}+\sqrt{(\log p/\loglog p)^{2}\cdot\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}/m_{r}^{3}})
, for
L(\mathbf{U})\equiv\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}
, the associated bias
\Bias_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})\equiv L({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\check{\mathbf{U}}])-L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}})
and variance
\Var_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})\equiv{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[L(\check{\mathbf{U}})]-L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}})
, in a spirit similar to those of subsampled OLS in
Definition

4.1
, where we define
\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}=(\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},1}+\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},2})^{2}+\sqrt{\epsilon\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}})}(\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},1}+\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},2})
, with
\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},1}=\|\diag\{\sqrt{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C})/m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{B}_{1}\|_{F}
and
\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},2}=\|\diag\{\sqrt{\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})/m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}\}^{p}_{j=1}\mathbf{B}_{2}\|_{F}
for
\mathbf{B}_{1}=(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times p}
and
\mathbf{B}_{2}=(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times n}
,
\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times k_{c}}
left singular vector of
\mathbf{C}
, and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times k_{r}}
right singular vector of
\mathbf{R}
.
Here,
\mathbf{c}_{i}^{\top}
is the
i^{\text{th}}
row of
\mathbf{C}
,
\mathbf{r}_{j}^{\top}
is the
j^{\text{th}}
row of
\mathbf{R}^{\top}
,

\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})
and
\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})
as in
Definition

2.3
denote the maximum importance sampling approximation
factors for
\mathbf{C}
and
\mathbf{R}^{\top}
, respectively.

F.1
Detailed proof of Theorem
F.1

We first recall some notations.
Let
k_{c}=\rm{rank}(\mathbf{C})\leq c
and
k_{r}=\rm{rank}(\mathbf{R})\leq r
.

For
\mathbf{C}
, consider its singular value decomposition (SVD)

\mathbf{C}=\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}
, where
\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times k_{c}}
and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{c\times k_{c}}
contain the left and right singular vectors of

\mathbf{C}
, respectively, and
\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}=\diag(\sigma_{\mathbf{C},1},\ldots,\sigma_{\mathbf{C},k_{c}})
, where
\sigma_{\mathbf{C},1}\geq\sigma_{\mathbf{C},2}\geq\ldots\geq\sigma_{\mathbf{C},k_{c}}
are its nonzero singular values. Similarly, the SVD of
\mathbf{R}
is
\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}
, where
\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times k_{r}}
and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times k_{r}}
contain the left and right singular vectors of
\mathbf{R}
, and
\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}}=\diag(\sigma_{\mathbf{R},1},\ldots,\sigma_{\mathbf{R},k_{r}})
, where
\sigma_{\mathbf{R},1}\geq\sigma_{\mathbf{R},2}\geq\ldots\geq\sigma_{\mathbf{R},k_{r}}
are its nonzero singular values.
We rewrite

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{C}\mathbf{C}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{R}^{\dagger}\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}\check{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}.

Following the proof strategies developed in
Theorem

4.4
and
Theorem

3.1
, the proof of
Theorem

F.1
proceeds in two main steps:

1.

construct a high probability event
\zeta
, based on subspace-embedding-type results from
Lemma

A.3
, under which the matrices
(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{-1}
and
(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{-1}
are well conditioned; and

2.

conditioning on that event
\zeta
, bound the quantities
\Bias_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})
and
\Var_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})-\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}
using a “leave-one-out” type analysis.

For random sampling matrix
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}
in
Algorithm

1
, denote
\mathbf{z}^{\top}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}=\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s_{c}}}/\sqrt{\pi_{\mathbf{C},i_{s_{c}}}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k_{c}}
the
i_{s_{c}}^{th}
row of the sketch
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}
, where
\{\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}\}^{n}_{i=1}
are the sampling probabilities of
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}
, so that
{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}]=\mathbf{W}^{\top}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}
. Similarly, for random sampling matrix
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}
in
Algorithm

1
, denote
\mathbf{z}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}=\mathbf{e}^{\top}_{i_{s_{r}}}/\sqrt{\pi_{\mathbf{R},i_{s_{r}}}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k_{r}}
the
i_{s_{r}}^{th}
row of the sketch
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}
, where
\{\pi_{\mathbf{R},i}\}^{p}_{i=1}
are the sampling probabilities of
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}
, so that
{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}]=\mathbf{V}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}
.
Without loss of generality, assume that
t_{c}=m_{c}/4
and
t_{r}=m_{r}/4
are integers, and define the event
\zeta=\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}\bigcap\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}
, where
\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}
depends only on
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}
and
\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}
depends only on
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}
, constructed as in (
14
). Specifically, for
\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}
, we set

\displaystyle\zeta_{\mathbf{C},j}:\sum^{t_{c}j}_{s_{c}=t_{c}(j-1)+1}\frac{1}{t_{c}}\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}\succeq\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}},~~~j=1,2,3,4,~~~\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}=\bigcap^{4}_{j=1}\zeta_{\mathbf{C},j},

(29)

and analogously, for
\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}
,

\displaystyle\zeta_{\mathbf{R},j}:\sum^{t_{r}j}_{s_{r}=t_{r}(j-1)+1}\frac{1}{t_{r}}\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}\succeq\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}},~~~j=1,2,3,4,~~~\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}=\bigcap^{4}_{j=1}\zeta_{\mathbf{R},j}.

(30)

Thus, we have, on
\zeta_{\mathbf{C},j}

\displaystyle\sum^{t_{c}j}_{s_{c}=t_{c}(j-1)+1}\frac{F_{i_{s_{c}}i_{s_{c}}}}{t_{c}}\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}\succeq\sum^{t_{c}j}_{s_{c}=t_{c}(j-1)+1}\frac{1}{t_{c}}\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{C},s_{c}}\succeq\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}},~~~j=1,2,3,4,

and
similarly, on
\zeta_{\mathbf{R},j}
,

\displaystyle\sum^{t_{r}j}_{s_{r}=t_{r}(j-1)+1}\frac{F_{i_{s_{r}}i_{s_{r}}}}{t_{r}}\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}\succeq\sum^{t_{r}j}_{s_{r}=t_{r}(j-1)+1}\frac{1}{t_{r}}\mathbf{z}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}{\mathbf{z}}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R},s_{r}}\succeq\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}},~~~j=1,2,3,4.

Since random sampling matrices
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}
and
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}
are independent, the events
\zeta_{\mathbf{C},1}
,
\zeta_{\mathbf{C},2}
,
\zeta_{\mathbf{C},3}
,
\zeta_{\mathbf{C},4}
,
\zeta_{\mathbf{R},1}
,
\zeta_{\mathbf{R},2}
,
\zeta_{\mathbf{R},3}
, and
\zeta_{\mathbf{R},4}
are independent.

In this following, we begin by bounding
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{C}\check{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{R}]-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}-\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}
.
Note that conditioned on the event
\zeta
,
\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}
and
\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}}
have full column rank, and
\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}
and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}
have full row rank. Together with
Lemma

A.2
, we further rewrite

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}\check{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{R}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\Sigma_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{R}}\Sigma_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}.

Considering

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}=\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})+\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})=\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X})-\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle+\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})=\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})+\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})=\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

and

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{C}\check{\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{R}]-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}=\tr({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}])

\displaystyle-\tr(({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}]\mathbf{X})-\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}])

\displaystyle+\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})=\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}({\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}]\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle+\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X})=\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})^{\dagger}]\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})

\displaystyle-\tr(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})+\tr(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}),

we have

\displaystyle\Bias_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})=\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle+\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}+\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle+2\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|_{F}

\displaystyle\cdot\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|_{F}

\displaystyle+2\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|_{F}

\displaystyle\cdot\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|_{F}

\displaystyle+2\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|_{F}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|_{F}.

Recalling the result in (
2
) in
Theorem

3.1
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}=O\left(\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}\right)\|(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}\right)\|(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}=O\left(\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}

and

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|^{2}_{F}=O\left(\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F},

where we use the inequalities

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-\mathbf{I}_{n})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}\leq\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F},~~\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}-\mathbf{I}_{p})\|^{2}_{F}\leq\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}.

We also similarly obtain

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}}[\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}]{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}}[(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}\right)\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}}[(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log n\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})c^{3}r^{3}}{(\loglog n\loglog p)^{2}m_{c}^{3}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log n\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})c^{3}r^{3}}{(\loglog n\loglog p)^{2}m_{c}^{3}m_{r}^{3}}\right)(\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|^{2}_{F}+\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle+2\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|_{F}\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\|_{F})

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{(\log n\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})c^{3}r^{3}}{(\loglog n\loglog p)^{2}m_{c}^{3}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F},

where in the first equality we take
\zeta=\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}\cap\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}
, with
\zeta_{\mathbf{C}}
and
\zeta_{\mathbf{R}}
independent.
Thus, we conclude

\displaystyle\Bias_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})=O\left(\frac{(\log n\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})c^{3}r^{3}}{(\loglog n\loglog p)^{2}m_{c}^{3}m_{r}^{3}}+\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}\sqrt{\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}\sqrt{\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}}+\frac{\log n\log p}{\loglog n\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})c^{3}r^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}m_{r}^{3}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}+\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}.

Subsequently, we proceed to bound the another term
\Var_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})-\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}
. We first rewrite

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})={\mathbb{E}}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{X}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}+\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}]

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\cdot\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}]

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+2\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}]}

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]}

\displaystyle+2\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}]}

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]}

\displaystyle+2\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}]}

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]}.

Using the result (
3
) in
Theorem

3.1
, it follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}}}\right)\|(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\right\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\right\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}}}+\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c}{m_{c}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]={\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log p}{\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{m_{r}^{3}}}\right)\|(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}}\Big\}^{p}_{j=1}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\right\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log p}{\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{m_{r}^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}}\Big\}^{p}_{j=1}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\right\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log p}{\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{m_{r}^{3}}}+\frac{\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r}{m_{r}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F},

where we apply the inequalities

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-\mathbf{I}_{n})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}\leq\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F},~~\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}-\mathbf{I}_{p})\|^{2}_{F}\leq\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}.

Recalling (
3
) in
Theorem

3.1
again, we similarly obtain,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+\frac{4\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c}{m_{c}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}].

We further similarly gain,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})^{\dagger}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log p}{\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{m_{r}^{3}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}\left[\left\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}}\Big\}^{p}_{j=1}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log p}{\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{m_{r}^{3}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}]

\displaystyle+\frac{4\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r}{m_{r}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\zeta}[\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}(\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\dagger}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}].

Putting the above together, we conclude

\displaystyle\Var_{\zeta}(\check{\mathbf{U}})=O\left(\frac{\log n}{\loglog n}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{m_{c}^{3}}}+\frac{\log p}{\loglog p}\sqrt{\frac{\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{m_{r}^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle+\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\right\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}}\Big\}^{p}_{j=1}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\right\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle+O\left(\sqrt[4]{\frac{(\log n)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}}{(\loglog n)^{2}m_{c}^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|_{F}\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}}\Big\}^{p}_{j=1}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle+O\left(\sqrt[4]{\frac{(\log p)^{2}\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}}{(\loglog p)^{2}m_{r}^{3}}}\right)\|\mathbf{C}\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{X}\|_{F}\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}}\Big\}^{p}_{j=1}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}\right\|_{F}\left\|\diag\Big\{\frac{(\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}))^{1/2}}{\sqrt{m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}}\Big\}^{n}_{i=1}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}\right\|_{F}.

This completes the proof.

F.2
Special cases of Theorem
F.1
under leverage score sampling and SRHT

In this section, we establish fine-grained accuracy characterizations for fast CUR under leverage-score sampling and the SRHT.
The proofs follow directly by combining the arguments from
Theorem

4.4
,
Corollary

4.5
, and
Corollary

4.7
, and are therefore omitted.

Corollary F.2

(Bias–variance characterizations for fsat CUR under leverage score sampling)
.

Under the settings and notations of
Theorem

F.1
, standard random sampling matrices
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{c}\times n}
with sampling probabilities

\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}\in[\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C})/(c\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})),\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C})/(c\theta_{\min}(\mathbf{C}))]
with
\theta_{\min}(\mathbf{C})\in[1/2,1]
and
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{r}\times p}
with sampling probabilities

\pi_{\mathbf{R},i}\in[\ell_{i}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})/(r\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})),\ell_{i}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})/(r\theta_{\min}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))]
with
\theta_{\min}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})\in[1/2,1]
, there exists
C>0
,
\alpha_{c}>0
,
\alpha_{r}>0
, independent of
n,p,c,r
so that for
m_{c}\geq C\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c\log(c/\delta)
,
m_{r}\geq C\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r\log(r/\delta)
,
\delta\leq\min\{n^{-(3+\alpha_{c})},p^{-(3+\alpha_{r})}\}
, when conditioned on an event
\zeta
that holds with the probability at least
1-\delta
, the standard sampled estimator
\tilde{\mathbf{U}}
in (
28
) satisfies

\displaystyle\Bias_{\zeta}(\tilde{\mathbf{U}})=\epsilon^{2}\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}),~\Var_{\zeta}(\tilde{\mathbf{U}})=\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}+\epsilon\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}),

with
\epsilon=O(\sqrt{(\log n/\loglog n)^{2}\cdot\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c^{3}/m_{c}^{3}}+\epsilon_{\theta,c}\cdot\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C})c/m_{c}+\sqrt{(\log p/\loglog p)^{2}\cdot\theta^{3}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r^{3}/m_{r}^{3}}+\epsilon_{\theta,r}\cdot\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})r/m_{r})
,
\epsilon_{\theta,c}=\max\{\theta^{-1}_{\min}(\mathbf{C})-1,1-\theta^{-1}_{\max}(\mathbf{C})\}
, and
\epsilon_{\theta,r}=\max\{\theta^{-1}_{\min}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})-1,1-\theta^{-1}_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top})\}
.
Here,
(\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{C}),\theta_{\min}(\mathbf{C}))
and
(\theta_{\max}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}),\theta_{\min}(\mathbf{R}^{\top}))
in
Definition

2.3
denote the maximum and minimum importance sampling approximation
factors for
\mathbf{C}
and
\mathbf{R}^{\top}
, respectively.

Corollary F.3

(Bias–variance characterizations for fast CUR decomposition under SRHT)
.

Under the setting and notations of
Theorem

F.1
,
let
\tilde{\mathbf{C}}_{\mathrm{SRHT}}=\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{C}_{\mathrm{HD}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{c}\times c}
the SRHT of
\mathbf{C}
with
\mathbf{C}_{\mathrm{HD}}=\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{C}/\sqrt{n}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}_{\mathrm{SRHT}}=\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{R}^{\top}_{\mathrm{HD}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{r}\times r}
the SRHT of
\mathbf{R}^{\top}
with
\mathbf{R}^{\top}_{\mathrm{HD}}=\mathbf{H}_{p}\mathbf{D}_{p}\mathbf{R}^{\top}/\sqrt{p}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{X}}_{\mathrm{SRHT}_{\mathbf{C},\mathbf{R}}}=\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{D}_{p}\mathbf{H}_{p}\mathbf{S}^{\top}_{\mathbf{R}}/\sqrt{np}
. Assume that
\min_{i,\|\mathbf{c}_{\mathrm{HD},i}\|>0}\|\mathbf{c}_{\mathrm{HD},i}\|^{2}/\|\mathbf{C}_{\mathrm{HD}}\|_{F}^{2}\geq n^{-{\alpha_{c}}}
and
\min_{j,\|\mathbf{r}_{\mathrm{HD},j}\|>0}\|\mathbf{r}_{\mathrm{HD},j}\|^{2}/\|\mathbf{R}_{\mathrm{HD}}\|_{F}^{2}\geq p^{-{\alpha_{r}}}
for some constants
\alpha_{c}>0
and
\alpha_{r}>0
.
Then, there exists
C>0
,
\max\{n\exp(-c),p\exp(-r)\}<\delta<\min\{n^{-(3+\alpha_{c})},p^{-(3+\alpha_{r})}\}
such that
for
m_{c}\geq Cc\log(c/\delta)
,
m_{r}\geq Cr\log(r/\delta)
, when conditioned on an event
\zeta
that holds with the probability at least
1-\delta
, the sampled estimator
\tilde{\mathbf{U}}=\tilde{\mathbf{C}}_{\mathrm{SRHT}}^{\dagger}\tilde{\mathbf{X}}_{\mathrm{SRHT}_{\mathbf{C},\mathbf{R}}}\tilde{\mathbf{R}}_{\mathrm{SRHT}}^{\dagger}
satisfies

\displaystyle\Bias_{\zeta}(\tilde{\mathbf{U}})=\epsilon^{2}\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}),~\Var_{\zeta}(\tilde{\mathbf{U}})=\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}+\epsilon\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}}),

with
\epsilon=O(\sqrt{(\log n/\loglog n)^{2}\cdot c^{3}/m_{c}^{3}}+\sqrt{c\log(n/\delta)}/m_{c}+\sqrt{(\log p/\loglog p)^{2}\cdot r^{3}/m_{r}^{3}}+\sqrt{r\log(p/\delta)}/m_{r})
,
where we define

\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR}}=(\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},1}+\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},2})^{2}+\sqrt{\epsilon\cdot L(\mathbf{U}_{\mathrm{CUR}})}(\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},1}+\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},2})
, with
\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},1}=\|\diag\{\sqrt{\ell_{i}(\mathbf{C}_{\mathrm{HD}})/m_{c}\pi_{\mathbf{C},i}}\}^{n}_{i=1}\mathbf{B}_{1}\|_{F}
and
\Delta_{\mathrm{CUR},2}=\|\diag\{\sqrt{\ell_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{\mathrm{HD}}^{\top})/m_{r}\pi_{\mathbf{R},j}}\}^{p}_{j=1}\mathbf{B}_{2}\|_{F}
for
\mathbf{B}_{1}=\mathbf{H}_{n}\mathbf{D}_{n}(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{C}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}/\sqrt{n}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times p}
and
\mathbf{B}_{2}=\mathbf{H}_{p}\mathbf{D}_{p}(\mathbf{I}_{p}-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\mathbf{X}^{\top}/\sqrt{p}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times n}
.
Here,

\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{c}\times n}
and
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{R}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m_{r}\times p}
are the uniform random sampling matrices applied to
\mathbf{C}_{\mathrm{HD}}
and
\mathbf{R}_{\mathrm{HD}}^{\top}
, respectively.

Table

2
summarizes the existing upper bounds on the bias of fast CUR decomposition established in prior work under uniform sampling, leverage score sampling, and SRHT
[

,

]
, and contrasts them with our results on debiased fast CUR decomposition.
We see that the proposed debiased approach consistently achieves a substantially smaller bias across all three sampling schemes.
